# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-09-18 through 2026-09-24  
**Updated:** 2026-09-18T10:54:21+00:00

## Best date plans

### 2026-09-18 — Red Hook Tavern + Illumination NYC at Wagner Park

**Dinner, then Illumination NYC at Wagner Park.** American tavern at Red Hook Tavern. It is 1.9 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Illumination NYC at Wagner Park starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=329+Van+Brunt+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&destination=20+Battery+Place%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10280&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.illumination.nyc) · [Restaurant](https://www.redhooktavern.com/)

### 2026-09-21 — Buck Wild Line Dancing at Domino Square + Nura

**Buck Wild Line Dancing at Domino Square, then dinner.** Indian-influenced New American at Nura. It is a 23-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=15+River+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&destination=46+Norman+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11222&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.dominopark.com/events/buck-wild-line-dancing-2) · [Restaurant](https://www.nurabk.com/)

### 2026-09-18 — Sofreh + Illumination Light Art Festival with DJs and Performances

**Dinner, then Illumination Light Art Festival with DJs and Performances.** Persian at Sofreh. It is 2.8 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Illumination Light Art Festival with DJs and Performances starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=75+Saint+Marks+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=20+Battery+Pl%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10280&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.illumination.nyc) · [Restaurant](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/)

[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=570+Vanderbilt+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&destination=1186+Broadway%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11221&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.wonderville.nyc/events/disasterpiece-movie-night-9-20-26) · [Restaurant](https://www.pattianns.com/)

#### [LUNGS Harvest Arts Festival in Loisaida Gardens](https://lungsnyc.org/harvest-arts-festival/)

6:00 PM · outdoors · Free · La Plaza Cultural and Loisaida community gardens · East Village · Manhattan · Score 75.0  
LUNGS closes the community-garden season with free art, poetry, puppets, workshops, theater, opera, dance, spoken word, and neighborhood garden events.
**Timing:** Festival runs September 18-27; official page highlights opening night at 6pm on September 18 and free events across Loisaida gardens.
**Practical note:** Mostly outdoor garden events; Sunday through Wednesday have rain chances in the NWS forecast, so check same-day updates.
**Why now:** The official 2026 festival opens September 18 and continues through September 27.

**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.

4:00 PM · markets · Free entry; most dishes capped at a few dollars · Queens Night Market · Corona · Queens · Score 54.6  
Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.
**Timing:** Saturdays 4pm to midnight. Note the seasonal gap between late August and mid September.
**Practical note:** Outdoors and cash-friendly; the adjacent lot charges for parking, so the 7 train is easier.
**Why now:** Runs every Saturday evening in season; the 7 train to 111th St drops you four blocks away.

## Where to eat

Verified against captured public evidence and 12 targeted follow-up checks; kept usable, dated or restaurant-specific items while rejecting exact duplicates and an unsplittable grouped heatmap card.

Dining research: 2026-09-18T10:37:44+00:00

Public MICHELIN page follow-up for El Califa de León returned no readable content. Eater states the Mexico City original was the world’s first Michelin-starred taqueria in 2024, but no NYC Michelin award is applied here; no unsupported Michelin-star claims are kept.

### Two-course weekday lunch prix fixe

Deals & happy hours — Portale

A practical weekday Chelsea lunch deal from Alfred Portale’s restaurant: two courses with antipasti and secondi choices.

Price: $45 2-course prix fixe; bistecca has a $15 supplement  
Schedule: Wednesday-Friday 11:30 AM-2:30 PM  
Conditions: Available Wednesday-Friday 11:30 AM-2:30 PM. Bistecca carries a $15 supplement; contorni and dessert are a la carte. Tax, tip, beverages and service charges not stated on the official menu. Source: https://www.portalerestaurant.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.portalerestaurant.com/menus/)

### Weekday bar happy hour

Deals & happy hours — Portale

Short early-evening bar-only happy hour useful before dinner in Chelsea.

Price: Happy-hour cocktails $14; wines by the glass $12; small bites $8-$24  
Schedule: Monday-Friday 5:00-6:30 PM  
Conditions: Monday-Friday 5:00-6:30 PM, exclusively at the bar. Small bites listed from $8 to $24; all happy-hour cocktails are $14 and wines by the glass are $12. Tax, tip and service charges not stated. Sources: https://www.portalerestaurant.com/menus/ and https://www.portalerestaurant.com/location/portale-restaurant/  
[Source](https://www.portalerestaurant.com/location/portale-restaurant/)

### Weekday happy hour

Deals & happy hours — Hav & Mar

Marcus Samuelsson’s Chelsea seafood restaurant lists weekday happy hour, useful around the Starrett-Lehigh/High Line area.

Schedule: Monday-Friday 5:00-7:00 PM  
Conditions: Official location page lists Happy Hour Monday-Friday 5:00-7:00 PM. Happenings page also lists Monday Martini Monday at the bar, 5-7 PM, with no reservation required. Exact food/drink prices, tax, tip and service charges not visible. Sources: https://www.havandmar.com/location/hav-mar/ and https://www.havandmar.com/happenings/  
[Source](https://www.havandmar.com/happenings/)

### Rise, Root & Revel 10-year anniversary dinner

Pop-ups — Hav & Mar

A one-night special dinner celebrating Rise & Root Farm’s 10th anniversary at Hav & Mar.

Schedule: 5:00-9:00 PM  
Conditions: September 27, 2026, 5:00-9:00 PM. Hav & Mar says tickets and additional details are through Rise & Root Farm; exact menu, ticket price, taxes, fees, tip and service charges were not visible in captured restaurant text. Source: https://www.havandmar.com/happenings/  
Event date: 2026-09-27  
[Source](https://www.havandmar.com/happenings/)

### NYCWFF brunch with Marcus Samuelsson and India Doris

Pop-ups — Hav & Mar

A bounded NYC Wine & Food Festival brunch hosted by Marcus Samuelsson and India Doris with Hav & Mar’s globally influenced seafood/seasonal cooking.

Schedule: 12:00-2:00 PM  
Conditions: October 18, 2026, 12:00-2:00 PM. Tickets are through NYCWFF; exact ticket price, taxes, fees, tip and service charges were not visible in captured Hav & Mar text. Source: https://www.havandmar.com/happenings/  
Event date: 2026-10-18  
[Source](https://www.havandmar.com/happenings/)

### Crown Shy lunch prix-fixe availability

Special menus — Crown Shy

A weekday Financial District lunch option from a high-end downtown restaurant; official page states a prix-fixe lunch menu is available.

Schedule: Lunch Monday-Wednesday 11:30 AM-2:00 PM; Thursday-Friday 11:30 AM-2:00 PM  
Conditions: Official menu says “Prix Fixe Menu Available” under lunch but captured text shows only a la carte lunch prices, not the prix-fixe price or full conditions. Tax, tip and service charges unknown. Sources: https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/ and https://www.crownshy.nyc/location/crown-shy/  
[Source](https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/)

### Jeffrey’s Grocery weekday daily additions

More restaurants — Jeffrey’s Grocery

A neighborhood seafood spot with day-specific dinner additions that make planning easier than a generic menu browse.

Price: Monday JG Burger $28; Tuesday whole fish tacos for two $65; Wednesday crispy fish sandwich $24; Thursday linguini & clams $28; Friday fish & chips $28  
Schedule: Monday-Friday daily additions; exact service hours not visible in fetched page text  
Conditions: Daily additions are regular menu items, not a discount: Monday JG Burger $28; Tuesday whole fish tacos for two $65; Wednesday crispy fish sandwich $24; Thursday linguini & clams $28; Friday fish & chips $28. Service hours, tax, tip and service charges not stated in captured menu text. Source: https://www.jeffreysgrocery.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.jeffreysgrocery.com/menus/)

### Bottomless mimosa brunch add-on

Deals & happy hours — Dagon

A clearly conditioned brunch add-on: bottomless mimosas with a one-hour limit and main-course purchase requirement.

Price: $24 bottomless mimosas  
Schedule: Saturday-Sunday 10:30 AM-3:00 PM brunch  
Conditions: Weekend brunch only, Saturday-Sunday 10:30 AM-3:00 PM. Bottomless mimosas are $24 per person, one-hour limit, with purchase of a main course; flavors listed as orange, peach and passionfruit. Tax, tip and service charges not stated. Source: https://www.dagonnyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.dagonnyc.com/menus/)

### Dagon regular-menu mezze bundles

More restaurants — Dagon

Shareable mezze bundles create a lower-cost way to sample several dips and small plates at brunch or dinner.

Price: Brunch mezze: 3 for $27 or 6 for $47; dinner mezze: 3 for $31 or 6 for $51  
Schedule: Brunch Saturday-Sunday 10:30 AM-3:00 PM; dinner Sunday-Thursday 5:00-10:30 PM and Friday-Saturday 5:00-11:00 PM  
Conditions: Regular online menu bundles: brunch/lunch mezze choice of 3 for $27 or 6 for $47; dinner mezze choice of 3 for $31 or 6 for $51. The page labels PDFs as samples and directs diners to online menus for current information. Tax, tip and service charges not stated. Source: https://www.dagonnyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.dagonnyc.com/menus/)

### New West Village French bistro reported open

New openings — Cafe Charmant

A new French-leaning West Village dinner option; official page confirms the address and Tuesday-Saturday dinner hours.

Schedule: Dinner Tuesday-Saturday 5:00-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Reported by The Infatuation as just added on September 17, 2026; actual opening date not independently stated on the official page. Official site confirms 340 Bleecker Street and dinner Tuesday-Saturday 5:00-10:00 PM. Sources: https://www.theinfatuation.com/new-york/guides/new-nyc-restaurants-openings and https://www.cafecharmantnyc.com/  
Published: 2026-09-17  
[Source](https://www.theinfatuation.com/new-york/guides/new-nyc-restaurants-openings)

## Discovery coverage

_No API key is needed for these public websites. `not checked` is distinct from a checked source with no final pick._

- **[arts] 92NY — blocked (fetch failed):** Events pages redirected to shared-session/stand-by pages and returned loading shell.

- **[arts] Atlantic Theater Company — blocked (fetch failed):** Assigned whats-on URL returned HTTP 404 during fetch.
- **[arts] Balance — used:** largest source: Anthology Film Archives 10/48; Brooklyn 13/48. Brooklyn below 40% because strongest verified weekday film/comedy calendars with structured data were Manhattan-heavy and several Brooklyn institutional calendars were blocked or sparse; borough counts Manhattan 34, Brooklyn 13, Queens…
